- compensation event + payment uplift but no incremental funding requirement; and
- project is bankable + authority cash obligations are unaffordable or unbudgeted.

- financial close achieved + first debt draw before outstanding conditions precedent are satisfied;
- forecast covenant breach + unchanged dividends despite contractual lock-up/cure path;
- contractor default + immediate project termination despite lender direct-agreement rights;
- insured loss + full immediate cash recovery despite deductible/waiting/reinstatement period;
- foreign-currency debt + CPI-only revenue index + zero FX/basis exposure without hedge;
- lifecycle actual spend below plan + unchanged asset condition and handback spend without evidence;
- senior debt fully repaid + lifecycle/handback spend collapses in the remaining concession period;
- renewable gross generation = billed energy with no availability/loss/curtailment adjustment;
- thermal plant fuel price pass-through + no penalty/cost for heat-rate deterioration;
- PPA expires + contracted tariff continues through merchant tail;
- heat-network addressable buildings = connected paying customers from day one; and
- construction delay + unchanged IDC/escalation/service start despite critical-path impact;
- financial-close model still uses feasibility-stage EPC/O&M/debt assumptions despite executed replacements;
- FX guarantee + unconstrained foreign-currency borrowing by the protected party;
- direct-agreement substitution right + zero replacement/mobilisation cost under contractor failure;
- tariff increase + assumed affordability/demand unchanged + no subsidy/support despite a material revenue gap;
- long-term fixed debt + early termination/refinancing + zero break cost;
- strategic sponsor sell-down + unchanged completion/operating support;
- renegotiated tariff/term/support + no bridge showing the value/risk transfer from the original concession;
- strong modelled DSCR + no credible lender capacity for the required amount/tenor/currency;
- preferred bidder declared affordable + financial-close adjusted payment exceeds the approved funding envelope;
- economic appraisal reports major user benefit + tariff revenue counted again as an independent social benefit without basis; and
- concession expiry/termination + zero authority transition/reprocurement cost despite an uninterrupted-service obligation.


- small incremental project O&M + existing network materially underfunded = public-sustainability contradiction;
- approved O&M budget + persistent low execution = maintenance-funded contradiction;
- project FIRR/FNPV passes + operator cannot fund network O&M/debt = project-viable/entity-unsustainable contradiction;
- 'self-financing' project + annual discretionary subsidy required to operate = intrinsic-viability contradiction;
- tariff assumed fully indexed + historic/regulatory process repeatedly delays or caps increases = tariff-achievability contradiction;
- covenant requires tariff increase + independent regulator controls approval = covenant-controllability contradiction;
- PPP option has lowest NPV + fails minimum required service = false-VfM contradiction;
- PPP option has strong VfM + exceeds credible authority budget = VfM/affordability contradiction;
- transferred risk explicitly added to PSC cash flow + same risk premium added to discount rate = potential risk-double-count contradiction;
- current PPP payment differs from financial-close payment + operating model has no index/deduction bridge = contract-administration contradiction;
- operating 'savings' + reduced lifecycle/maintenance + unchanged service/handback outcome = long-run-VfM contradiction;
- major contract change + no incremental financing source = change-funding contradiction;
- approaching expiry + large remedial obligation + negligible residual cash + no security = handback-funding contradiction;
- tolls remitted to treasury + road operator spends them directly = public-road cash-perimeter contradiction;
- geothermal project declared bankable + commercial resource not confirmed by drilling/feasibility evidence = resource-bankability contradiction;
- geothermal resource potential below required wellfield/plant output + no make-up-well/derating response = physical-capacity contradiction;
- geothermal available-capacity payment + revenue driven by dispatched energy only = PPA-payment contradiction; and
- take-or-pay/capacity PPA + unchanged cash under off-taker default = counterparty-credit contradiction.


- FOAK project + lender-grade capex precision + only early conceptual engineering = technology-maturity contradiction;
- pilot/component success + full integrated commercial plant performance assumed = scale-up/integration contradiction;
- TRL label treated as proof of bankability + no underlying operating evidence = technology-evidence contradiction;
- sponsor/project legal separation + essential IP/O&M rights terminate with sponsor distress = operational-separateness contradiction;
- "LSTK EPC" + technology risk carved out + low liability/security = false-wrap contradiction;
- take-or-pay revenue + unchanged cash under off-taker insolvency or off-spec product = counterparty/performance contradiction;
- technology insurance + same loss also fully recovered from EPC and warranty = recovery-double-count contradiction;
- permanent senior debt funds pre-close development + facility is unavailable until financial close = development-funding contradiction;
- historic/lifetime investor IRR healthy + negligible remaining shareholder cash + major unresolved rectification = false-distress-comfort contradiction;
- large authority deduction + subcontract pass-through assumed 100% + liability cap already exhausted = retained-SPV-risk contradiction;
- construction defect + contractor limitation/security expired + model still assumes full contractor recovery = latent-defect contradiction;
- underfunded DSRA/lifecycle reserve + unrestricted cash treated as stronger liquidity = reserve-distress contradiction;
- covenant waiver + model treats underlying breach as permanently cured = waiver-is-not-cure contradiction;
- late subcontractor payments / workout lender involvement + unchanged base-case operating forecast = distress-evidence contradiction;
- authority automatic subsidy plug + no legal/budget commitment + project called bankable = support-commitment contradiction;
- road heavy-maintenance expense smoothed annually + no cash funding in actual intervention year = lifecycle-cash contradiction;
- road traffic grows beyond physical/service capacity + no congestion/diversion/augmentation response = traffic-capacity contradiction;
- existing port terminal operating during new-terminal construction + model has zero revenue until new terminal COD = brownfield-phasing contradiction;
- transshipment discount applied to all container revenue + contract limits it to transshipment volumes = port-tariff-mix contradiction;
- port sponsor earns EPC margin + project model describes same affiliate margin as project saving = related-party-return contradiction; and
- port trustee/DSRA cash counted as unrestricted working capital while a revolver is also drawn = restricted-liquidity contradiction.


- early PPP scoping plugs user fees to required investor return + no affordability/demand response = reverse-scoping-as-revenue contradiction;
- do-minimum alternative meets the required service materially cheaper + PPP case ignores it at options stage = option-screening contradiction;
- project COD depends on authority access road/grid/utility/enabling work + no funded delivery path = enabling-infrastructure contradiction;
- mandatory environmental/community/consent mitigation + zero capex/opex/schedule effect = safeguard-financial disconnect;
- unresolved permit/consent is a debt draw/service-start condition + model assumes unconditional on-time draw/COD = approval-dependency contradiction;
- affiliate EPC/O&M/manager margin counted as both an SPV saving and an additional project return = related-party double-benefit contradiction;
- index-linked debt + indexed revenue + high-inflation scenario + no principal accretion/index-basis/lag effect = false-inflation-hedge contradiction;
- credit wrap/guarantee fee added + no pricing/tenor/debt-capacity/risk benefit + value still increases mechanically = unsupported-wrap-benefit contradiction;
- partial-risk or political-risk guarantee + model treats all project non-payment as guaranteed = guarantee-scope contradiction;
- financing lease asset + contractual handback + full terminal asset sale proceeds to SPV = ownership/residual-value contradiction;
- qualifying refinancing materially changes current sponsor distributions + gain sharing calculated from stale financial-close forecast = refinancing-baseline contradiction;
- refinancing releases sponsor equity/cash + contract has special extraction-sharing rule + ordinary gain share only = refinancing-extraction contradiction;
- hospital management PPP + private scope excludes clinical staff + model charges full private clinical payroll = hospital-scope contradiction;
- hospital availability-payment PPP + revenue modelled as patient volumes × tariff = hospital-payment-regime contradiction;
- social-health-insurer/public payer dominates hospital revenue + zero receivables/claim denials/payment lag = hospital-cash-conversion contradiction;
- hospital licence/accreditation expires + full reimbursable service/revenue continues unchanged = hospital-eligibility contradiction;
- medical-equipment downtime/ageing + unchanged diagnostic capacity/revenue and no replacement = hospital-equipment contradiction;
- public hospital staff fully transferred/replaced + legacy public payroll remains + new private payroll added = hospital-workforce double-count contradiction;
- contract-linked hospital quality failure + no cure cost/payment/default consequence = hospital-performance contradiction;
- management fee and revenue/profit share are alternative bid compensation methods + model pays both = hospital-compensation double-count contradiction;
- internal hospital-system transfer counted as a new admission at both sending and receiving sites = hospital-network-demand double-count contradiction;
- social-housing unit sold + same unit continues generating full rental income without leaseback/management right = housing-perimeter contradiction;
- project SPV cash trapped by covenants + holdco debt service assumes unrestricted upstream distributions = holdco-structural-subordination contradiction;


Additional toll-road traffic-risk contradictions from the World Bank/PPIAF/GIF traffic-risk framework:

- investment-grade traffic case + no documented survey/model maturity beyond a pre-feasibility screen = forecast-maturity contradiction;
- opening-year DSCR relies mainly on long-term development/induced traffic that cannot exist at opening = timing-of-demand contradiction;
- same new development counted in macro traffic growth and explicit development traffic = development-double-count contradiction;
- toll road base case + no separate existing/reassigned/diverted traffic logic for a material greenfield capture case = opening-day-risk opacity;
- traffic surveys collected in a known seasonal/event peak + annualised as neutral average with no adjustment = survey-annualisation contradiction;
- traffic model "validated" only against the same counts used to calibrate it = false-validation contradiction;
- network model omits a material competing route + still claims robust reassignment = network-completeness contradiction;
- toll increases materially + traffic capture unchanged + traffic study uses willingness-to-pay/diversion logic = toll-demand disconnect;
- financial model toll schedule differs from traffic-study toll schedule + no revised traffic case = cross-model tariff contradiction;
- one average willingness-to-pay value applied to passenger cars and heavy commercial traffic despite materially different user benefits = WTP-mix contradiction;
- ramp-up ends before user awareness/tolling system is operational + debt case assumes full mature traffic = ramp-up contradiction;
- macro growth + car ownership growth both drive traffic independently despite one already being embedded in the other = driver-double-count contradiction;
- future traffic depends on a complementary road opening + downside leaves that road on time = correlated-network contradiction;
- government promises road traffic risk is transferred + availability payment funded entirely from retained tolls + public low-traffic exposure ignored = retained-fiscal-risk contradiction;
- shadow toll described as user-revenue risk transfer + authority still bears toll collection/evasion/FX risk = traffic-versus-revenue-risk contradiction;
- shadow-toll bands exist + finance model uses one average toll across all vehicles = marginal-payment contradiction;
- minimum-revenue guarantee protects 100% of sponsor base-case return + contract only protects a defined revenue floor = over-guarantee contradiction;
- MRG improves bankability + sovereign payment credit is weaker than assumed and no payment-risk downside is run = guarantee-credit contradiction;
- MRG floor modelled + upside share omitted despite a linked cap-and-collar clause = asymmetric-risk-allocation contradiction;
- public subordinated traffic facility + model treats it as grant with no repayment/loss profile = public-credit-exposure contradiction;
- full user-pay concession classified as successful risk transfer + expected renegotiation restores downside losses to government = false-risk-transfer contradiction;
- flexible-term concession + original fixed expiry still drives debt/lifecycle/handback = variable-term contradiction;
- flexible-term revenue target + opening-year debt shortfall ignored because later term extension exists = liquidity-versus-duration contradiction;
- low traffic scenario + lower toll revenue + unchanged toll-linked O&M/lifecycle/capacity only where costs should respond = incomplete-traffic-downside contradiction;
- high traffic scenario + larger toll cash + no pavement wear, congestion or capacity response = traffic-upside-free-lunch contradiction;
- bidder with highest traffic forecast wins + no realism screen/lender diligence in a highly uncertain greenfield procurement = winner's-curse exposure;
- public traffic study, bidder study and lender traffic case materially differ + finance model gives no version bridge = traffic-case-governance contradiction;
- operating road actual traffic materially misses forecast + reforecast preserves the original growth path with no recalibration = outturn-evidence contradiction;



Additional contradiction patterns from the 2026 web-research pass:

- residual land value rises because the developer hurdle falls + same lower hurdle is then reported as evidence the land is more marketable = circular land-value contradiction;
- land valued on optimum/highest use + financial model develops a lower-density scheme + no special-assumption bridge = valuation-scope contradiction;
- planning approval is not secured + GDV assumes approved density + acquisition price paid as unconditional land value = entitlement contradiction;
- development margin is healthy + project IRR is weak solely because duration extends materially + model reports both as equivalent pass metrics = return-metric contradiction;
- cost-to-complete exceeds remaining committed funding + model still reports 'fully funded' because original sources and uses balanced = current-funding contradiction;
- interest reserve depleted by delay + new debt replenishes reserve + value/feasibility not re-tested = masked-ADC-deterioration contradiction;
- presales exceed lender threshold + majority are refundable reservations / subject to finance + model treats threshold as unconditional = presale-quality contradiction;
- physical completion achieved + occupancy below stabilization test + construction debt refinanced anyway = completion-versus-stabilization contradiction;
- lease executed + long free-rent period + model starts cash rent at execution = rent-commencement contradiction;
- slower lease-up + unchanged operating deficit / leasing commissions / finance carry = absorption-cost contradiction;
- lower exit value + unchanged LTV-based take-out debt = refinance-value contradiction;
- senior construction facility is cost-to-complete controlled + borrower diverts draw to unrelated land/site = draw-purpose contradiction;
- as-stabilized appraisal value used for current LTV + substantial remaining capex and lease-up still required = prospective-collateral contradiction;
- sponsor completion guarantee relied upon + sponsor portfolio liquidity stress ignored = guarantee-capacity contradiction;
- BESS provides full frequency reserve MW + same MW simultaneously dispatched for full arbitrage = storage-power-double-commitment contradiction;
- BESS begins period at minimum SOC + delivers large upward reserve activation + no prior charge/headroom = storage-SOC contradiction;
- battery degradation reduces MWh + contracted duration/MW maintained + no augmentation = storage-contract-capacity contradiction;
- battery cycles beyond warranty cap + warranty replacement still modelled in full = storage-warranty contradiction;
- hybrid plant export exceeds shared interconnection rating + no curtailment = hybrid-grid-cap contradiction;
- merchant ancillary-service price compression case + unchanged stacked revenue because availability fees are hard-coded = storage-market-downside contradiction;
- airport charge is per departing passenger + model applies to arriving and transfer passengers = airport-charge-unit contradiction;
- aeronautical cost base excludes commercial space cost + non-aero rent also assumes airport paid none of common facility cost = airport-common-cost contradiction;
- charge path materially above cost/regulatory framework + no approval/consultation risk = airport-tariff-rights contradiction;
- non-aeronautical retail revenue increases with passengers + concession rent is also percentage of the same retail turnover booked as separate airport sales = airport-commercial-double-count contradiction;
- E&S condition precedent outstanding + debt draw proceeds as scheduled = lender-ES-condition contradiction;
- resettlement budget omitted + site access date unchanged despite unresolved acquisition = E&S-access contradiction;
- climate adaptation capex funded by project debt + debt capacity unchanged despite no extra project cash flow = resilience-debt-capacity contradiction;
- resilience measure produces public avoided-loss benefit + project CFADS includes the same avoided loss as cash revenue = resilience-benefit-cash contradiction;
- resilience hardening reduces expected outage + insurance premium/deductible also falls + both benefits claimed with no insurer evidence = resilience-double-benefit contradiction;
- disaster scenario assumes insured reinstatement + financing waterfall distributes insurance proceeds to equity before repair/lender control = insurance-control contradiction;

# Infrastructure dependency chain

```text
Project legal/contract/regulatory structure
  → defines asset/service scope and risk allocation
  → defines revenue/payment regime
     → user-pay / availability / usage-linked government pay / regulated / contracted capacity / hybrid
        → determines which demand, availability, tariff and credit risks affect project cash flow

Land/site/right-of-way + design + permits + construction packages
  → interface completion
  → commissioning/testing/acceptance
  → service commencement
  → recurring operating revenue/payment

Demand drivers
  → chargeable physical units
  → tariff/charge basis
  → gross billed revenue
  → deductions/leakage/credit/collection
  → cash revenue

Physical asset capacity + operating constraints + maintenance outages
  → practical service capacity
  → utilisation/congestion/service level
  → augmentation/lifecycle trigger
  → capex + downtime
  → future capacity/service

Routine O&M + asset usage + age/condition
  → lifecycle/major maintenance
  → asset availability/performance
  → payment deductions / user demand / operating cost
  → residual condition
  → handback/remediation

Inflation/index formula + cost exposure + contractual lag
  → service/tariff adjustment
  → operating/lifecycle cost escalation
  → margin / cash-flow exposure

Relief/change/force majeure event
  → physical/cost/schedule impact
  → contract compensation/time extension/pass-through/share
  → net project cash-flow impact

Government support / grant / guarantee
  → contractual trigger
  → project cash inflow or risk mitigation
  → possible clawback/upside sharing/termination interaction

Climate hazard
  → physical damage/capacity/downtime
  → adaptation/repair/insurance
  → service/payment/revenue
  → lifecycle and handback

PPP financial model / project finance:
contract + bid solution + financing package
  → agreed base case financial model
  → construction sources/uses and funding sequence
  → operating cash flow
  → finance-document CFADS
  → debt service / reserve funding
  → distribution tests / trapped cash
  → equity distributions

contract change / compensation event
  → incremental scope/cost/schedule effect
  → incremental funding requirement and financing cost
  → contractual compensation mechanism
  → updated payment/tariff/term / residual project economics

refinancing
  → changed margin/tenor/leverage/reserves/hedges
  → refinancing gain under contract definition
  → authority share + shareholder residual benefit

post-close operating model:
financial-close/base case → financial-close-equivalent calibration of operating structure
  → actual historic P&L / cash flow / balance sheet
  → actual debt / reserve / shareholder cash-flow history
  → current remaining reforecast
  → total-contract current outcome + lender/shareholder reporting

secondary-market sponsor interest:
actual sponsor instruments + remaining forecast distributions/sub-debt cash flows + residual obligations
  → hold value / required return
  ↔ net sale or acquisition consideration + transaction / financing effects

Cross-adviser / model authority:
executed project + finance + technical + demand + cost/QS + insurance evidence
  → authoritative assumptions by model purpose/date
  → technical/contractual bridge
  → financial model base case / current forecast

Funding availability:
conditions precedent + equity funding + security/insurance + draw certificates
  → usable debt commitment
  → construction draw
  → completion / service commencement

Distress / lender protection:
project or subcontract default
  → notice / cure / cash trap / sponsor support where applicable
  → lender step-in / substitution under direct agreements
  → only then termination/enforcement where rights are exhausted

Construction programme:
critical path + package interfaces + long-lead items + testing/acceptance
  → period capex + escalation + IDC + LD/support exposure
  → COD/service commencement

Operating asset condition:
base-case lifecycle → actual maintenance/renewal → current condition
  → remaining lifecycle + availability risk
  → post-debt period obligations
  → handback/remediation

Public fiscal:
direct government commitments + contingent guarantees/support
  → trigger/timing/quantum
  → annual public cash exposure + downside/termination exposure

Authority payment funding:
contractual availability/service/capital obligations
  → approved budget / appropriation / long-term funding mechanism
  → invoice/certificate and payment due date
  → authority cash payment
  → SPV receivable / cash / DSCR

Authority economic appraisal:
without-project physical/service counterfactual
  → incremental with-project physical impacts
  → economic resource costs + user/third-party benefits/externalities under governing methodology
  → economic NPV/EIRR
  ↔ separate bridge to tariff/subsidy/financial sustainability and sustained O&M/lifecycle service

Construction delay recovery:
critical-path delay
  → gross lost/deferred revenue + IDC/escalation/extended project cost
  → contractual LD entitlement + cap/exclusions/relief/security
  → collection timing / dispute
  → net retained project loss + liquidity/covenant impact

Live project risk:
risk register / technical and contract-management evidence
  → current status + mitigation + owner
  → schedule/service/cost consequence
  → contractual relief/recovery
  → residual current-forecast / downside exposure

Power:
resource/dispatch + plant availability/efficiency + grid availability
  → gross generation → losses/auxiliary/curtailment → saleable energy
  → PPA capacity/availability + energy/merchant payments
fuel/resource + heat rate/efficiency → variable input cost
contract expiry → merchant tail / decommissioning if applicable

District energy:
connected load + seasonality/diversity → heat demand + peak
  → network losses + plant/backup dispatch → input energy
  → metered/billed heat × tariff/index → cash collection

Rail:
traffic unit → train service/path/fleet requirement → network/terminal capacity → fare/freight/access revenue
track access cost to operator ↔ access revenue to infrastructure manager → eliminate only in integrated group

Toll road:
O-D demand + network alternatives + toll → class traffic → chargeable transactions → collections
traffic + axle loading → pavement/lifecycle → maintenance/capacity expansion

Airport:
passenger/aircraft/cargo mix → aero charges + non-aero spend → revenue
traffic mix → runway/stand/terminal bottleneck → expansion trigger

Port:
trade/customer demand → vessel calls/throughput → berth/crane/yard/gate/hinterland capacity → port/terminal revenue

Water:
source → treatment → system input → NRW → billed consumption → tariff → collection

Regulated network:
eligible capex → RAB/RAV → return/depreciation + opex/incentives/true-ups → allowed revenue → tariff/cash recovery

Social infrastructure:
asset availability/performance → deductions → unitary/service payment
lifecycle spend → availability + residual condition → handback

Digital:
premises passed/addressable market → take-up/contracted capacity → active service → recurring/contract revenue
network utilisation + technology cycle → upgrade/augmentation capex

Waste:
waste received → recovery/process/reject mass balance → gate/service/by-product revenue
residual tonnes → landfill airspace → cell/closure/post-closure obligations

Early PPP scoping:
service need / MPSS + do-minimum / alternative technical solution
  → rough capex + O&M/lifecycle + finance requirement
  → approximate required revenue
  → feasible user charges / public payment / support
  → residual commercial gap / redesign / no-go decision

enabling infrastructure:
retained authority / third-party access-road, utility, grid, land or intermodal scope
  → funded delivery programme
  → project access / completion / demand dependency
  → contractual relief / support if late
  → project COD and cash-flow consequence

environmental / social / consent obligation:
applicable law / lender standard / project agreement
  → assessment + consultation/consent + permit + mitigation
  → capex/opex/land/schedule/monitoring
  → drawability / construction / service commencement / operating compliance

project-finance instrument choice:
project CFADS/risk + market capacity + contract term
  → feasible bank / bond / index-linked / asset-based / guarantee-supported structures
  → draw/prefunding + debt-service profile + reserves/covenants + break/refinance cost
  → project payment requirement + downside resilience + sponsor cash flow

hospital / health-service PPP:
catchment + disease/referral profile + competing supply + payer coverage
  → service demand / case mix + capacity / length of stay
  → billable activity by payer + claim eligibility
  → reimbursement / collection lag + bad debt
  → operating cash and working capital
clinical/admin/equipment/staff obligations
  → service availability + quality/accreditation
  → contract payment / remediation / cost / default effect

```


Additional public-infrastructure and operating-PPP dependency chains:

```text
Public asset / GGSU recurrent-service sustainability
  technical/normative network O&M + asset condition/backlog + replacements
  → unconstrained recurrent requirement
  → budget request / appropriation
  → cash release and execution
  → maintenance actually performed
  → asset condition / service quality / economic life
  → future rehabilitation or renewal need

Public utility / operator sustainability
  service volume + tariff policy + billing/collection + technical/commercial losses
  → operating cash generation
  + subsidy/compensation actually received
  → O&M + lifecycle + existing debt/capital obligations
  → network financial sustainability
  → capacity to sustain the new project

Project financial evaluation — where governing methodology requires it
  with-project cash flow − without-project cash flow
  → real incremental project cash flow on prescribed perimeter
  → project financing-plan cost of capital / prescribed threshold
  → FNPV / FIRR or equivalent viability result
  → switching values / coherent risk scenarios
  ↔ separate operator/entity sustainability and economic appraisal

PPP VfM decision
  equivalent output/service requirement
  → conventional/reference option + PPP option
  → whole-life cost + allocated/valued risks
  + explicit non-valued effects
  → affordability screen
  → VfM comparison / procurement decision
  → procurement competition / bid sanity check
  → ex-durante/ex-post realised VfM review where required

Operational PPP administration
  contractual base payment/tariff
  → indexation + availability/performance + pass-through/true-up
  → compensation/change/refinancing adjustments
  → invoice/payment cash
  → actuals + current forecast
  → future authority commitment / SPV sustainability
  → termination/expiry settlement and handback security

Geothermal project
  surface studies / reservoir concept
  → exploration + confirmation/test drilling
  → commercial resource and well productivity
  → production/injection well count + gathering system
  → deliverable wellfield capacity
  → gross plant output − auxiliary/losses
  → net PPA capacity / energy
  → capacity + energy revenue
  → resource decline / plant degradation
  → make-up wells + plant overhaul
  → sustained contracted output and CFADS
```

# Domain-specific graded tests (T2-S10-285 to T2-S10-787)

These tests add infrastructure-specialist challenges to the existing core audit framework. They do not replace
generic formula, evidence, financing, reporting or governance procedures.

### T2-S10-285 — test: infrastructure_revenue_regime_classification
Classify each material project revenue stream as user-pays, government availability, usage-linked government
payment, regulated revenue, contracted capacity/offtake or hybrid. Confirm the model's demand, tariff,
availability and collection sensitivities match the actual regime rather than the project label.

### T2-S10-286 — test: infrastructure_payment_performance_link
Where project revenue is performance-linked, trace the relevant service/asset performance measures into the
payment calculation. Confirm underperformance reduces or adjusts payment according to the contract rather
than remaining a narrative KPI.

### T2-S10-287 — test: infrastructure_availability_deduction_logic
For an availability-payment project, reconcile unavailable unit/area, criticality, duration, performance points,
relief events, multipliers and caps/no-caps to the contractual deduction mechanism. Test at least one material
service-failure case.

### T2-S10-288 — test: infrastructure_indexation_cost_exposure
Reconcile indexed/fixed portions of tariff or service payment to the actual index, base date, lag, frequency and
underlying project cost exposure. Identify unsupported 100% CPI indexation or flat nominal revenue where the
contract provides escalation.

### T2-S10-289 — test: infrastructure_risk_allocation_model_consistency
For each material infrastructure-specific risk, identify the contractual bearer and any relief, compensation,
pass-through, insurance, sharing or tariff-reset mechanism. Confirm the model captures the **net economic
exposure** and avoids duplicate risk charges.

### T2-S10-290 — test: infrastructure_construction_service_commencement
Distinguish construction completion, commissioning, acceptance and service commencement. Confirm recurring
service/user revenue, deductions, LDs and contractual milestones start at the dates applicable to each mechanism.

### T2-S10-291 — test: infrastructure_lifecycle_renewal_funding
Reconcile long-term asset/component lives to routine maintenance, periodic maintenance, major renewals and any
contractual lifecycle/renewal reserve. Confirm lifecycle spend is sufficient to support modelled service levels over
the concession/forecast period.

### T2-S10-292 — test: infrastructure_handback_residual_condition
Where handback applies, reconcile required residual life/condition, pre-expiry surveys, remediation, reserve/bond
and transition obligations to final-period capex/opex. Reject an unsupported end-of-term maintenance holiday.

### T2-S10-293 — test: infrastructure_change_relief_rebalancing
For material change-in-law, compensation, relief, variation or force-majeure scenarios, apply the contract's
actual sharing, time-extension, pass-through or economic-rebalancing mechanism rather than assuming the project
bears or recovers 100% of gross cost.

### T2-S10-294 — test: infrastructure_termination_compensation
Where termination value is material, distinguish termination causes and test the applicable compensation basis,
debt treatment, deductions, insurance/break costs and timing. Do not use one generic terminal payment for all
causes.

### T2-S10-295 — test: infrastructure_government_support_contingent_triggers
Reconcile grants, VGF, subsidies, minimum-revenue support, guarantees, land/in-kind contributions and other
public support to their contractual trigger and period. Confirm support is not double counted or treated as
unconditional where contingent.

### T2-S10-296 — test: infrastructure_capacity_bottleneck_consistency
Identify the binding physical/operational bottleneck across the asset system and confirm modelled throughput
cannot exceed practical system capacity. Tie capacity expansion to the relevant trigger, lead time, capex and
commissioning response.

### T2-S10-297 — test: infrastructure_outside_view_cost_schedule_demand
For material greenfield/megaprojects, compare project-specific cost, schedule and demand assumptions with a
credible relevant reference class where available. Require explanation for unusually favourable positioning;
do not mechanically impose generic historical uplifts.

### T2-S10-298 — test: infrastructure_climate_resilience_economic_propagation
Where physical climate exposure is material, trace hazard into asset damage/capacity/downtime, repair/adaptation,
insurance/relief, service-payment or user-revenue consequences and lifecycle/handback. Avoid generic climate
haircuts detached from asset physics.

### T2-S10-299 — test: infrastructure_land_site_resettlement_schedule
For projects with material land/right-of-way/resettlement dependencies, reconcile staged site availability,
compensation/relocation work, utility relocation and lender/permit conditions to the construction schedule and
cost. Test entitlement to relief where delays are allocated to another party.

### T2-S10-300 — test: infrastructure_demand_revenue_risk_correlation
In demand scenarios, preserve causal links between volume, price/elasticity, variable O&M, wear, collection and
capacity/lifecycle capex. Reject cases that capture only revenue downside/upside while freezing directly linked
physical consequences.

### T2-S10-301 — test: rail_traffic_unit_and_revenue_reconciliation
For rail, reconcile passengers versus passenger-km and tonnes versus tonne-km/train-km as applicable. Confirm
revenue tariffs are applied to the correct traffic unit and journey/haul-length assumptions.

### T2-S10-302 — test: rail_track_access_and_consolidation
Where track access applies, reconcile access rights/train paths/usage to access charges. If payer and recipient are
within one integrated group, confirm internal access economics eliminate while genuine external access revenue
remains.

### T2-S10-303 — test: rail_network_capacity_train_path_constraint
Test forecast rail traffic against practical train-path/network capacity, including signalling/headways, junctions,
loops, stations/terminals, possessions and speed mix where material. Route-km alone is not a capacity test.

### T2-S10-304 — test: rail_rolling_stock_availability_and_maintenance
Reconcile service plan to fleet size, consist capacity, technical availability, maintenance/overhaul downtime,
utilisation and cycle time. Confirm traffic growth is physically deliverable by the rolling-stock plan.

### T2-S10-305 — test: rail_traction_mix_energy_response
Where electric/diesel traction mix changes, trace the change into electricity/fuel consumption, relevant fleet and
maintenance, traction-power/electrification capex and service capability as applicable.

### T2-S10-306 — test: rail_subsidy_public_service_obligation_logic
For subsidised passenger/public-service rail, identify the subsidy/PSO payment basis and reconcile it to service
volume, eligible cost/revenue gap, performance, indexation and review terms. Do not use subsidy as an unlimited
plug unless contractually cost-plus/gap-filling.

### T2-S10-307 — test: rail_integrated_separated_structure_consistency
Where rail structure can switch between integrated and separated entities, reconcile economic perimeter,
external versus internal access revenue, shared costs and subsidies so each activity appears once in the selected
view.

### T2-S10-308 — test: rail_performance_disruption_regime
Where track-access or service contracts contain delay/disruption/performance incentives, trace reliability and
network-performance assumptions into applicable compensation, penalties or bonuses.

### T2-S10-309 — test: tollroad_od_traffic_and_rampup
For a material greenfield/newly tolled road, reconcile traffic to origin-destination evidence, existing counts,
network development and a distinct ramp-up period. Confirm ramp-up is not double counted with underlying
long-run traffic growth.

### T2-S10-310 — test: tollroad_toll_elasticity_vehicle_mix
Reconcile traffic by vehicle/user class to class tolls, discounts/exemptions and willingness-to-pay/elasticity where
material. Test whether toll changes produce a plausible demand response rather than only a mechanical price
increase.

### T2-S10-311 — test: tollroad_competing_network_diversion
Identify existing/planned competing or complementary routes and test their effect on traffic. Where non-compete
or network-change protection exists, reconcile any contractual compensation/relief separately from the traffic
impact.

### T2-S10-312 — test: tollroad_collection_leakage
Bridge physical traffic to chargeable transactions and cash collections through exemptions, discounts, evasion,
enforcement, bad debt and transaction costs where material.

### T2-S10-313 — test: tollroad_payment_model_traffic_risk
Classify road revenue as real toll, shadow toll, availability/performance or hybrid and confirm traffic and user-
collection risk are assigned consistently. Do not apply real-toll revenue mechanics to an availability road.

### T2-S10-314 — test: tollroad_lifecycle_capacity_maintenance
Reconcile traffic/vehicle mix and engineering assumptions to pavement/structure maintenance, major renewals,
closures and any capacity augmentation. High-traffic upside should not leave wear and capacity capex unchanged
without support.

### T2-S10-315 — test: airport_aeronautical_revenue_build
Reconcile each material aeronautical charge to its correct passenger, aircraft movement, aircraft weight/type,
parking, cargo or other charging base and the applicable tariff/regulatory mechanism.

### T2-S10-316 — test: airport_nonaeronautical_revenue_build
For material non-aeronautical revenue, reconcile retail/F&B/parking/property/advertising and other streams to
passenger mix, spend, commercial area/capacity, concession terms and timing rather than applying one passenger
growth factor to all revenue.

### T2-S10-317 — test: airport_traffic_airline_mix_concentration
Reconcile passenger/aircraft/cargo forecasts to airline, route, domestic/international and O&D/transfer mix where
material. Test concentration downside coherently across traffic, receivables and non-aero revenue.

### T2-S10-318 — test: airport_capacity_trigger_consistency
Identify binding runway, taxiway, stand, terminal, security/border, baggage or access constraint and reconcile
traffic growth to actual expansion trigger, programme and incremental capacity.

### T2-S10-319 — test: airport_charge_setting_indexation
Where aeronautical charges are regulated/consulted/cost-recovery based, reconcile tariff updates, indexation,
review periods and any single/dual/hybrid-till or equivalent jurisdictional mechanism to the applicable framework.

### T2-S10-320 — test: airport_airline_collection_credit
For material airline receivables, reconcile billing and collection terms, concentration, deposits/security and
credit/default assumptions. Passenger traffic alone does not guarantee cash collection from airlines.

### T2-S10-321 — test: port_throughput_revenue_and_concession_fee
Reconcile port/terminal throughput by appropriate unit to handling, wharfage, marine, storage and other charges,
then reconcile any concession rent/royalty/throughput fee so it is neither omitted nor double counted across the
operator/authority perimeter.

### T2-S10-322 — test: port_multibottleneck_capacity
Test port throughput against channel/berth, crane, yard/storage, gate, labour, equipment and other practical
constraints. Confirm capex addresses the actual binding bottleneck rather than relying on one headline terminal
capacity.

### T2-S10-323 — test: port_hinterland_interface
Where material, reconcile port/terminal growth to connecting rail, road, barge/pipeline or logistics capacity and
operating interface. Port-side capacity alone is insufficient where cargo cannot enter/leave at forecast rates.

### T2-S10-324 — test: port_dredging_depth_maintenance
Where draft/depth matters, reconcile vessel assumptions to channel/berth depth, capital and maintenance
dredging, responsibility, downtime and environmental constraints.

### T2-S10-325 — test: port_line_customer_concentration
Identify material shipping-line/alliance, commodity, mine/industrial customer or transshipment concentration and
reconcile contract tenure/minimum commitments to downside scenarios.

### T2-S10-326 — test: port_productivity_turnaround_capacity
Reconcile throughput growth to berth occupancy, crane/productivity rates, vessel turnaround, yard dwell and
operating hours where these are material capacity drivers.

### T2-S10-327 — test: water_balance_nrw_revenue
Reconcile water produced/purchased to system input, authorised/billed consumption and NRW before applying
tariffs. Confirm system-input volume is not treated as revenue volume unless the commercial arrangement truly
pays on bulk input.

### T2-S10-328 — test: water_billing_collection_separation
Bridge billed volume and tariff to bills issued and cash collection through collection efficiency and timing. Test
billing improvement separately from collection improvement.

### T2-S10-329 — test: water_tariff_subsidy_affordability
Reconcile tariff classes/blocks, fixed and volumetric components, indexation/review, subsidies and collection to
the actual regulatory/contractual framework. Do not assume unrestricted tariff increases where approval is
required.

### T2-S10-330 — test: water_source_treatment_network_capacity
Trace source/abstraction, treatment, storage, pumping/transmission and distribution constraints to saleable water
capacity. Confirm capex relieves the binding part of the chain and associated energy/chemical use responds.

### T2-S10-331 — test: wastewater_collection_treatment_sludge
For wastewater assets, reconcile sewer inflow including project-specific infiltration/inflow and return-to-sewer
assumptions to pumping, treatment, discharge and sludge/biosolids handling capacity and cost.

### T2-S10-332 — test: water_nrw_capex_performance
For an NRW-reduction programme, reconcile investment/operating measures to physical-loss and/or commercial-
loss reduction and trace benefits to additional billing, reduced production cost, released capacity or deferred
capex without double counting.

### T2-S10-333 — test: regulated_network_rab_rollforward
Where a RAB/RAV regime applies, reconcile opening regulatory asset base, eligible additions, regulatory
depreciation, disposals/indexation/adjustments and closing balance. Keep regulatory asset base distinct from
accounting PPE.

### T2-S10-334 — test: regulated_network_allowed_revenue_build
Reconcile allowed revenue to the regulator's actual building blocks — allowed return, depreciation, opex, tax,
incentives, pass-throughs, true-ups and inflation/other adjustments as applicable — rather than a generic cost-plus
calculation.

### T2-S10-335 — test: regulated_network_incentive_output_trueup
Trace service/output incentives, overs/underspend sharing, prior-period revenue corrections and other regulator-
specified true-ups into the correct future recovery periods.

### T2-S10-336 — test: regulated_network_volume_exposure_regime
Classify the regulatory control as revenue cap, price cap, volume-linked or other mechanism and test how demand
changes affect allowed and collected revenue, including lagged true-ups where applicable.

### T2-S10-337 — test: regulated_network_reopener_adjustment
For material reopener/uncertainty mechanisms, identify trigger, approval/prudency test, eligible spend, timing and
recovery. Confirm the model does not assume automatic full RAB/revenue recovery for every actual cost.

### T2-S10-338 — test: social_infra_availability_payment
For social infrastructure, reconcile maximum unitary/service payment to service commencement, availability and
performance rather than occupancy/demand unless the contract explicitly transfers that demand risk.

### T2-S10-339 — test: social_infra_service_deduction_criticality
Test deductions by affected service/area, criticality, duration, repeat failure and relief/cure mechanics where
contractually relevant. Confirm critical-service outages are not averaged away by total building availability.

### T2-S10-340 — test: social_infra_soft_services_repricing
Where soft services are included, reconcile fixed/indexed/pass-through components and contractual market-
testing/benchmarking/repricing dates to future service costs and payment adjustments.

### T2-S10-341 — test: social_infra_lifecycle_handback
Reconcile component lives, lifecycle renewals and any reserve to availability standards and required residual
condition at handback. Confirm final-period maintenance does not fall below what handback requires.

### T2-S10-342 — test: digital_takeup_capacity_revenue
For broadband/fibre, bridge premises/sites passed and serviceable premises to connected/active customers or
contracted wholesale capacity and then to revenue. Do not equate network reach with paying take-up.

### T2-S10-343 — test: digital_wholesale_contract_vs_forecast
Separate committed wholesale/capacity contracts from forecast merchant/take-up demand. Reconcile minimum
commitments, contract tenor, churn/renewal and customer concentration to the base and downside cases.

### T2-S10-344 — test: digital_iru_lease_recurring_revenue
Distinguish upfront IRU/connection payments, dark-fibre/capacity leases and recurring access/service charges.
Reconcile cash timing and contractual service period without treating upfront and recurring economics as
interchangeable or duplicative.

### T2-S10-345 — test: digital_redundancy_upgrade_obsolescence
Reconcile installed capacity to saleable capacity after redundancy/resilience requirements, then link utilisation
and technology cycles to active-equipment upgrades/augmentation. Do not apply passive-asset life to all network
components.

### T2-S10-346 — test: waste_tonnage_gatefee_scope
Reconcile contracted/merchant waste tonnage by stream to collection, transfer, processing and disposal scope and
the applicable service/gate/take-or-pay payment. Avoid charging the same tonne twice for overlapping scope.

### T2-S10-347 — test: waste_diversion_airspace_closure
Reconcile incoming waste through recycling/recovery/reject mass balance to residual landfill tonnes and airspace.
Tie cell-development, progressive/final closure and post-closure obligations to physical usage and timing.

### T2-S10-348 — test: waste_byproduct_revenue_reconciliation
For energy/recyclate/compost/carbon or other by-product revenue, reconcile recoverable yield, quality, price,
contractual ownership and processing/disposal cost. Prevent gross theoretical yield or duplicate product revenue.

### T2-S10-349 — test: ppp_contractual_basecase_model_role
Where the financial model is contractually relevant, identify authority, bid, financial-close, contractual base-case and
current-forecast models. Confirm each is used for its intended purpose and that current forecasting has not destroyed
the agreed baseline needed for contractual adjustments.

### T2-S10-350 — test: ppp_contract_to_model_economic_mapping
Trace material payment, indexation, change, relief, refinancing, termination, lifecycle and handback provisions from
the executed PPP contract into model economics. Confirm formulas reproduce the operative commercial effect rather
than merely carrying similarly named assumptions.

### T2-S10-351 — test: ppp_financial_close_rebase
Compare winning-bid assumptions with executed financing and project contracts at financial close. Confirm actual debt
quantum, margins, fees, hedging, equity funding, D&C/O&M terms and service-commencement conditions are reflected
where the close model is intended to represent executed economics.

### T2-S10-352 — test: ppp_construction_sources_uses_timing
Reconcile construction sources and uses by period, including debt/equity timing, grants, IDC, financing fees, reserve
prefunding and contingency. Identify any temporary funding deficit hidden by a lifetime sources-and-uses equality.

### T2-S10-353 — test: ppp_equity_funding_and_completion_support
Check the required equity injection sequence, shareholder/subordinated funding, equity bridge and contingent
completion/cost-overrun support against finance documents and construction cash needs. Do not assume optional sponsor
support beyond committed terms.

### T2-S10-354 — test: ppp_cfads_finance_document_definition
Reconstruct CFADS from the applicable finance definition and confirm the model includes/excludes tax, working capital,
lifecycle, reserve movements, compensation receipts and other material items consistently before calculating lender
coverage ratios.

### T2-S10-355 — test: ppp_debt_capacity_and_sculpting_constraints
Where debt is cash-flow sized or sculpted, confirm debt quantum and amortisation respect the applicable DSCR/LLCR or
other lender tests, maturity, leverage/equity, reserves, downside case and concession-tail constraints. Do not impose
external generic thresholds.

### T2-S10-356 — test: ppp_debt_tail_concession_alignment
Compare final scheduled senior-debt repayment with concession/payment expiry and any required lender tail. Confirm
cash flows supporting debt service remain legally and operationally available for the full debt period.

### T2-S10-357 — test: ppp_reserve_account_economics
For each contractual or financing reserve, verify target balance, funding, permitted draw, replenishment, interest,
release and termination/refinancing/expiry treatment. Confirm restricted reserve cash is not treated as freely
distributable cash.

### T2-S10-358 — test: ppp_distribution_lockup_and_trapped_cash
Where finance documents impose distribution tests, verify shareholder distributions stop when tests/default conditions
fail and that trapped cash is retained, swept, reserved or released exactly as the financing specifies.

### T2-S10-359 — test: ppp_bid_to_close_financing_adjustment
Where financing economics are adjusted between bid and financial close, separate base-rate movement, lender margin,
fees and hedging. Confirm only the risks retained or shared by the authority are neutralised through the contractual
adjustment.

### T2-S10-360 — test: ppp_interest_rate_hedge_debt_alignment
Where interest-rate hedging is material, reconcile hedge notional and tenor to debt exposure and test early
refinancing/termination breakage economics where contractually relevant.

### T2-S10-361 — test: ppp_refinancing_gain_share
For a contractual refinancing-gain mechanism, reproduce the prescribed before/after economics including fees, hedge
breakage, reserve and leverage effects, then allocate the authority and shareholder shares through the required payment
or cash mechanism without double counting.

### T2-S10-362 — test: ppp_refinancing_dependency
Where project viability depends on refinancing or a mini-perm takeout, model refinancing timing, available tenor,
pricing, debt capacity and downside/failed-refinancing consequences rather than assuming an automatic rollover.

### T2-S10-363 — test: ppp_change_compensation_funding
For material authority changes or compensation events, connect incremental capex/opex/lifecycle/schedule effects to
the incremental funding requirement and financing cost, then apply the actual contractual compensation mechanism.

### T2-S10-364 — test: ppp_financial_rebalancing_metric
Where the contract requires financial rebalancing after a qualifying event, identify the exact protected metric or
calculation basis and verify the model preserves that contractual position rather than an assumed generic equity IRR.

### T2-S10-365 — test: ppp_subcontract_risk_pass_through
Compare material PPP obligations against D&C/O&M subcontract protection for dates, standards, deductions, liability
caps, relief events, indexation, lifecycle and handback. Quantify retained SPV exposure where pass-through is
incomplete.

### T2-S10-366 — test: ppp_authority_affordability_and_contingent_cashflow
Where the model serves the procuring authority, separately forecast government capital contributions, service
payments, guarantees/support, retained costs, compensation events, termination exposure and handback/transition
obligations. Do not infer public affordability from private bankability.

### T2-S10-367 — test: infrastructure_authoritative_document_maturity
For each material contractual/technical/financing assumption, identify the evidence that is authoritative for the
model purpose and date. Confirm executed/final terms have not been silently replaced by earlier bid, indicative or
management assumptions where those terms were superseded.

### T2-S10-368 — test: infrastructure_cross_adviser_reconciliation
Reconcile material legal/commercial, technical, demand/resource, cost/QS, insurance and financing adviser outputs to
the financial model. Investigate differences in dates, scope, capacity, performance, cost, risk allocation or support
rather than assuming the workbook is the source of truth.

### T2-S10-369 — test: infrastructure_technical_model_financial_bridge
Where a technical/operational model feeds the financial model, bridge physical outputs to financial inputs and test
that aggregation does not remove economically material seasonality, peak, outage, loss, curtailment or commissioning
constraints.

### T2-S10-370 — test: ppp_conditions_precedent_drawability
For material debt facilities, identify first-draw and subsequent-draw conditions that can affect construction funding.
Confirm debt is not drawn before required equity, project effectiveness, permits, insurance, security, hedging or
other applicable conditions are satisfied.

### T2-S10-371 — test: ppp_drawstop_undrawn_commitment
Where a default/drawstop or failed draw condition can block future funding, confirm undrawn commitment is not treated
as automatically available and that the project has a credible cure/support/replacement funding path.

### T2-S10-372 — test: ppp_multitranche_financing_intercreditor
For materially different financing tranches, reconcile currency, availability, draw order, maturity, amortisation,
prepayment/sweep, reserves, ranking and refinancing treatment. Confirm synthetic blending does not hide a tranche-
specific funding or maturity constraint.

### T2-S10-373 — test: ppp_covenant_breach_cure_waiver
For a forecast financing breach, follow the actual contractual sequence through lock-up/cash trap, reserve or equity
cure, waiver, prepayment, acceleration or enforcement as applicable. Do not jump directly from ratio breach to
termination.

### T2-S10-374 — test: ppp_sponsor_support_limit
Reconcile completion support, contingent equity, standby debt or other sponsor cure to committed amount, expiry, call
conditions and purpose. Confirm the model does not assume unlimited discretionary sponsor support.

### T2-S10-375 — test: ppp_direct_agreement_stepin_sequence
Where lenders have direct agreements, reconcile notice, cure, step-in, substitution/novation and relevant time periods
to default and termination scenarios. Confirm termination cash flow is not triggered before lender rights are
exhausted where those rights are exercisable.

### T2-S10-376 — test: ppp_credit_support_recoverability
For PCGs, bonds, LCs, LDs, guarantees, standby support and similar protections, model the applicable cap, expiry, call
condition, recovery timing and counterparty. Prevent face-value contractual support from being treated as immediate
unconditional cash.

### T2-S10-377 — test: infrastructure_insurance_recovery_economics
For a material insured event, reconcile cover, limit, deductible, waiting period, business interruption, reinstatement
and use of proceeds to the project downside. Confirm insurance does not eliminate retained downtime or cash-flow loss.

### T2-S10-378 — test: ppp_insurance_premium_sharing
Where a PPP contract has an insurance premium risk-sharing/review mechanism, reproduce the actual base-versus-actual
cost, indexation, review period and sharing rules rather than applying generic inflation or full pass-through.

### T2-S10-379 — test: infrastructure_counterparty_credit_concentration
Identify material authority/offtaker/customer, EPC/O&M, supplier, insurer and grid/network counterparty exposures.
Where project economics rely on one or a small number of counterparties, test the contractual support and cash-flow
consequence of delayed/defaulted performance or payment.

### T2-S10-380 — test: infrastructure_fx_index_basis_risk
Bridge local/foreign revenue, capex, opex and debt to contractual FX/index pass-through and hedges. Quantify residual
lag, partial-indexation, cap/floor or basis exposure rather than assuming a CPI or FX clause creates a perfect hedge.

### T2-S10-381 — test: ppp_public_fiscal_direct_contingent
For an authority-side model, separate committed public payments from contingent guarantees/support. For each material
contingent liability, model trigger, timing, basis, cap, duration and interaction with project cash flows.

### T2-S10-382 — test: ppp_public_sector_option_equivalence
Where a PSC/reference/public-sector option is required, confirm it provides equivalent service quality, output, timing
and whole-life maintenance to the private-finance option and applies the governing jurisdiction's risk, tax and
financing conventions consistently.

### T2-S10-383 — test: ppp_ifric12_concession_classification
Where IFRS statutory statements and IFRIC 12 are in scope, reconcile grantor control/residual-interest conditions and
the operator's rights to the financial-asset, intangible-asset or mixed model. Keep accounting classification distinct
from economic demand-risk allocation.

### T2-S10-384 — test: infrastructure_construction_programme_financial_bridge
Reconcile critical path, float, interfaces, long-lead items, testing and acceptance to period capex, escalation, IDC,
LDs/support and service commencement. Confirm delay scenarios move only the costs/revenues causally affected.

### T2-S10-385 — test: infrastructure_construction_cost_contingency_stack
Reconcile committed contract price, owner costs, escalation/FX, allowances, interface/commissioning costs, quantified
risks, contingency and financing carry. Detect duplicated contingency/risk allowances or omitted cost scope.

### T2-S10-386 — test: infrastructure_remaining_cost_to_complete
For a partly constructed project, reconcile actual spend, committed remaining work, claims/variations, remaining
contingency and schedule to current cost-to-complete and remaining funding. Do not apply an original-total-capex
overrun percentage to already spent/fixed costs.

### T2-S10-387 — test: infrastructure_lifecycle_actuals_condition_reconciliation
For an operating asset, reconcile original lifecycle plan to actual maintenance/renewal, current condition/defects and
remaining lifecycle/handback works. Determine whether apparent underspend is efficiency, deferral or under-
maintenance.

### T2-S10-388 — test: infrastructure_post_debt_handback_discipline
Where senior debt matures before concession expiry, separately test remaining lifecycle, reserves/security,
distributions, service performance and handback obligations after debt repayment. Confirm debt-free years do not
create an unsupported maintenance holiday.

### T2-S10-389 — test: infrastructure_correlated_downside_dependencies
Build at least one material downside around causal dependencies — such as delay/IDC/LDs, outage/insurance/deductions,
or low traffic/debt cover/augmentation — and confirm linked variables move coherently rather than as isolated or
mutually inconsistent shocks.

### T2-S10-390 — test: power_ppa_revenue_component_classification
For a power project, classify PPA/offtake revenue into capacity/availability, energy/output, take-or-pay, support and
merchant components. Confirm each component responds to the contractual availability, dispatch, volume and price
conditions that actually govern it.

### T2-S10-391 — test: power_cod_testing_acceptance
Reconcile EPC completion, grid energisation, performance/reliability testing, PPA COD and final acceptance to revenue,
LDs, debt milestones and operating obligations. Do not use one completion date for all triggers unless contractually
correct.

### T2-S10-392 — test: renewable_resource_generation_loss_stack
Bridge renewable resource to gross generation and then through availability, technical/electrical losses, auxiliary
load and curtailment to metered/saleable energy. Reconcile probability/resource cases to the supporting technical
study where available.

### T2-S10-393 — test: thermal_heat_rate_fuel_cost
For thermal generation, reconcile net generation, heat rate/efficiency, auxiliary load, fuel quantity, fuel price/index
and logistics. Confirm fuel-price pass-through does not also compensate project-controlled efficiency shortfall unless
the PPA says so.

### T2-S10-394 — test: power_dispatch_curtailment_deemed_energy
Separate project outage, resource shortfall, economic dispatch, grid constraint and offtaker curtailment. Apply deemed
energy/generation compensation only to events, thresholds and measurement rules eligible under the PPA.

### T2-S10-395 — test: power_grid_interconnection_dependency
Reconcile interconnection/transmission completion, connection capacity and grid availability to plant COD and saleable
energy. Where grid delay/constraint is not project risk, apply the actual contractual relief/compensation rather than
assuming physical output can be sold.

### T2-S10-396 — test: power_degradation_outage_overhaul
Reconcile degradation, forced/planned outage and major overhaul/replacement to generation, availability payment, O&M
and lifecycle capex across the asset life.

### T2-S10-397 — test: power_offtaker_credit_support
Where revenue depends on an offtaker/utility, reconcile payment security, LC/liquidity support, government guarantee
or other credit enhancement to exposure, duration and call mechanics. Do not treat a fixed tariff as equivalent to
creditworthy cash.

### T2-S10-398 — test: power_merchant_tail_support_expiry
Where PPA/support expires before technical asset life, separate contracted and merchant periods. Confirm merchant
prices, dispatch/curtailment and market exposure replace — rather than inherit — expired contractual protections.

### T2-S10-399 — test: power_decommissioning_restoration
Where project-borne, reconcile decommissioning/restoration/recycling obligations, timing, security/funding and residual
asset/salvage assumptions to concession/lease expiry and technical end of life.

### T2-S10-400 — test: heat_network_energy_balance_peak_capacity
For a heat/cooling network, bridge connected/contracted load through seasonality/diversity, peak demand, network
losses and plant/backup dispatch to heat sold and input energy. Confirm annual averages do not bypass peak-capacity or
seasonal constraints.

### T2-S10-401 — test: heat_network_connection_offtake_ramp
Separate addressable buildings, signed/committed customers, connection timing, connected capacity, actual offtake and
take-or-pay/minimum volume. Prevent pipeline demand from being treated as contracted cash revenue.

### T2-S10-402 — test: heat_network_tariff_energy_basis_risk
Reconcile heat tariff/fixed-charge/indexation to fuel/electricity/carbon exposure, reset dates and caps/floors. Quantify
retained index/commodity basis risk and connect plant/network efficiency to variable input cost.

### T2-S10-403 — test: infrastructure_assumption_maturity_convergence
For each material project assumption, identify its maturity state at the model date — feasibility estimate, adviser base
case, market-tested term, binding quote/commitment, executed contract or operating actual/current evidence. Confirm a
late-stage model uses the most mature applicable source without overwriting a contractual base case that remains legally
relevant.

### T2-S10-404 — test: ppp_financing_certainty_flexibility_breakcost
Where financing is long-tenor fixed-rate, heavily hedged or otherwise expensive to unwind, reconcile prepayment,
make-whole, hedge close-out and related break costs to refinancing, variation, sale and termination scenarios. Compare
financing structures on both affordability and flexibility where the difference is material.

### T2-S10-405 — test: ppp_replacement_contractor_practicality
For a material contractor/operator distress scenario with step-in or substitution rights, test whether a qualified replacement
is practically available and model procurement/approval time, mobilisation, transition cost, replacement pricing and
recoveries where material. Do not make legal substitution economically instantaneous.

### T2-S10-406 — test: ppp_contract_mechanism_representative_event
Run at least one representative worked event through each materially valuation-sensitive contractual mechanism that would
otherwise remain dormant in the base case, such as payment deductions, tariff review, benchmarking, compensation,
refinancing gain share or termination. Confirm thresholds, lags, caps, sharing and cure logic reproduce the contract.

### T2-S10-407 — test: userpay_tariff_service_affordability_support_loop
For a material user-pay concession/utility, reconcile service scope and whole-life cost/financing requirement to tariff,
affordability/willingness to pay, demand/collection and any residual subsidy/VGF/support. Prevent tariff from acting as an
unsupported plug independent of customer response and public affordability.

### T2-S10-408 — test: ppp_government_guarantee_risk_profile
For a material government guarantee, model contractual maximum exposure and a proportionate view of expected/severe
downside exposure and timing. Where data and materiality justify it, use probability-weighted, stochastic, option-style or
other appropriate valuation as a cross-check; do not require complex methods when the evidence cannot support them.

### T2-S10-409 — test: ppp_risk_decision_right_alignment
For a material transferred/shared risk, identify which party controls the decisions that change exposure or sensitivity to that
risk. Confirm the contract/model contains approvals, caps, sharing or retained-risk mechanisms that prevent a protected
party from increasing another party's exposure without economic consequence.

### T2-S10-410 — test: ppp_common_vs_bidder_owned_assumptions
Where procurement prescribed common assumptions for bid comparability, distinguish those assumptions from bidder-owned
commercial risks. At financial close, reconcile which values remain contractual/common, which are reset, and which have
been superseded by executed supplier/financing terms.

### T2-S10-411 — test: ppp_sponsor_retention_equity_transfer
Where strategic sponsor/operator equity retention or transfer restrictions apply, reconcile permitted sell-down dates/levels to
completion support, guarantees, operating capability and distributions/refinancing. Confirm sponsor support is not assumed to
survive a transfer unless the obligation actually does.

### T2-S10-412 — test: ppp_renegotiation_value_risk_bridge
For a material concession renegotiation/restructuring, preserve the pre-renegotiation baseline and bridge all revised tariff/
payment, term, support, debt, equity and risk-allocation changes to the post-renegotiation economics. Quantify the resulting
value/risk transfer between authority, users, lenders and shareholders.

### T2-S10-413 — test: ppp_adjustment_vs_renegotiation_classification
Classify material post-close changes as pre-agreed contractual adjustment, contractual compensation/rebalancing, or true
renegotiation. Apply the correct economic baseline and entitlement; do not treat a discretionary concession as an automatic
contractual right or vice versa.

### T2-S10-414 — test: ppp_credit_support_private_downside_retention
Where government guarantees, debt underpinning or other credit support protects lenders/investors, identify the portion of
capital genuinely exposed to project performance and the contingent public exposure. Confirm the model does not equate
heavy protection with unchanged private risk transfer or standalone project-cash-flow bankability.

### T2-S10-415 — test: ppp_payment_remedy_no_double_recovery
For a material service/performance failure, map payment deductions, performance points, LDs/damages and other financial
remedies to the contract's interaction/no-double-remedy rules. Confirm the same economic loss is not recovered twice and
that a distinct remedy is not incorrectly suppressed merely because another mechanism also applies.

### T2-S10-416 — test: ppp_persistent_breach_default_sequence
Where persistent-breach/default provisions are material, reproduce the warning, final-warning, cure/recurrence periods,
lender cure/step-in and termination sequence. Confirm repeated minor failure neither triggers premature termination nor
remains forever limited to small deductions when the contract provides an escalation path.

### T2-S10-417 — test: ppp_payment_finance_charge_ringfence
Where revenue is service/performance based, confirm scheduled debt service, interest or finance charges are not carved out
from deductions or guaranteed by the payment mechanism unless the contract expressly provides that protection. Reconcile
any protected finance component to the actual risk allocation and public support.

### T2-S10-418 — test: ppp_reviewable_service_benchmark_market_test
For each material reviewable service, reconcile review dates, benchmarking/market-test method, efficient alternative provider
or bid evidence, permitted profit/margin, escalation to an alternative value-testing method, transition cost and resulting
service-payment reset. Do not model the review as an ordinary CPI uplift unless that is the contractual method.

### T2-S10-419 — test: ppp_public_capital_private_risk_ratio
Where government capital contribution/VGF is paid during construction, reconcile payment to actual milestone achievement and
any contractual base-case private/public funding relationship. Confirm delayed private debt/equity does not cause public cash
to fund ahead of the agreed risk position and that equity-bridge timing does not eliminate equity intended to remain at risk.

### T2-S10-420 — test: ppp_insurance_reinstatement_economic_test
For a material catastrophic insured event, identify whether insurance proceeds automatically reinstate the asset or are subject
to an economic reinstatement/use-of-proceeds test. Where a test exists, reproduce its metric, timing and consequences; do not
substitute an annual DSCR snapshot for a contractually defined whole-life/loan-life measure.

### T2-S10-421 — test: ppp_uninsurable_risk_cashflow
Where a PPP has an uninsurability regime, reconcile the loss of required cover to saved-premium/service-payment adjustment,
periodic market re-testing, re-procurement if cover returns, and authority/project cash consequences if the event occurs. Do not
assume full pass-through or full project retention without the contractual mechanism.

### T2-S10-422 — test: ppp_default_termination_market_valuation_route
For private-party default where compensation depends on market value, determine whether the contract requires/allows a liquid-
market retender or no-retender/expert valuation. Reconcile route selection, settlement timing, interim service cash flows,
deductions, asset condition and lender step-in/substitution to the termination value.

### T2-S10-423 — test: ppp_termination_debt_form_settlement_route
For a material termination, classify bank senior debt, bond/capital-market debt, genuinely debt-like subordinated instruments
and equity-like shareholder debt under the project agreement. Reconcile break gains/costs, mitigation and any option to
transfer/assume senior debt rather than repay it immediately in cash.

### T2-S10-424 — test: ppp_handback_retention_escrow
Where pre-expiry survey/handback mechanics permit service-payment withholding, escrow/retention or lifecycle-surplus capture,
reconcile funding, remediation spend, reimbursement, authority self-procurement and residual release. Prevent double counting
with an SPV handback reserve unless both forms of security are required.

### T2-S10-425 — test: ppp_psc_competitive_neutrality_cashflow
Where the governing PSC methodology includes competitive neutrality, trace each adjustment to a jurisdiction-specific
ownership-related cash-flow advantage/disadvantage. Exclude generic public/private efficiency assumptions and non-cash items
unless the governing method expressly requires them.

### T2-S10-426 — test: ppp_contract_ambiguity_model_uncertainty
For a material payment, deduction, indexation, lifecycle, compensation, refinancing or termination term that is contractually
ambiguous or disputed, identify competing plausible interpretations and any established operating practice/resolution process.
Do not embed one interpretation as certain economics; quantify an alternative/downside value case where the uncertainty is
material and unresolved.



### T2-S10-427 — test: userpay_variable_term_revenue_target
Where a user-pay concession uses least-present-value-of-revenue or another contractual revenue-target / variable-term mechanism,
recalculate cumulative eligible discounted revenue using the contract's revenue definition, index/discount rule and adjustments.
Confirm the resulting concession expiry (including any min/max term) drives debt maturity/tail, lifecycle, handback, distributions
and terminal economics rather than remaining hard-coded to the original expected date.

### T2-S10-428 — test: ppp_revenue_floor_cap_sharing
Where minimum-revenue support, revenue floors, caps or upside sharing apply, reproduce the mechanism as one integrated demand-risk
allocation. Reconcile eligible revenue, floor/cap triggers, sharing percentages, true-ups/clawbacks, aggregate limits, settlement
lag and any effect on tariff, concession term or termination; prevent overlapping downside support and upside calculations.

### T2-S10-429 — test: ppp_renegotiation_expected_rescue_lowballing
Where the base case or investment case relies on a future tariff increase, term extension, subsidy, scope relief or other material
post-award concession, determine whether it is an existing contractual/legal entitlement or merely anticipated renegotiation.
Bridge original bid/close economics to the assumed revised bargain and do not treat discretionary future relief as bankable base-case
cash flow.

### T2-S10-430 — test: ppp_claims_disputes_forecast_treatment
For each material unsettled claim/change event, distinguish notice/event, asserted entitlement, time relief, amount claimed,
certified/agreed amount, disputed balance, expected determination/settlement timing and contractual interest/indexation/carry.
Reconcile delayed cash to bridge funding/covenants and prevent disputed amounts from being recognised as certain cash or credible
authority exposure from being omitted.

### T2-S10-431 — test: ppp_design_build_maintain_incentive_alignment
Where the model attributes value to integrated design-build-maintain/operate procurement or a material value-engineering saving,
trace the design/capex decision through operating efficiency, maintenance, lifecycle, availability/service deductions and handback.
Confirm the party benefiting from the saving bears enough of the downstream consequence for the claimed whole-life incentive to exist.

### T2-S10-432 — test: ppp_public_support_cash_vs_risk_value
Where an authority appraisal claims to value transferred risk, guarantees or contingent support economically, distinguish contractual
maximum exposure, expected cash loss by period, severe downside and the risk-adjusted/fair-value measure required by the governing
methodology. Do not equate zero near-term expected cash with zero economic cost or impose a fair-value basis where the jurisdiction
requires another treatment.

### T2-S10-433 — test: road_pbc_service_level_payment
For an output/performance-based road contract, reconcile service-level/road-condition KPIs, measurement periods, cure rules and
payment reductions to the management-and-maintenance payment. Confirm contractor intervention/maintenance choices can affect both
cost and payment and that the model does not remunerate the service component solely from input quantities.

### T2-S10-434 — test: road_pbc_work_category_risk_allocation
Where the road contract separately provides management/maintenance services, rehabilitation, improvement and emergency works,
classify each category by pricing basis, instruction/approval, performance condition and risk allocation. Confirm ordinary deterioration
or contractor failure is not converted into separately reimbursable emergency/improvement work and that measured-work cash flows are
not confused with service-level lump-sum payments.

### T2-S10-435 — test: ppp_completion_test_support_release
For construction-phase sponsor/completion support, identify the finance-defined release test rather than assuming release at physical
PC/COD. Reconcile independent certification, technical/performance/reliability requirements, funded punch-list/cost-to-complete, reserve
funding and any finance-defined ratios/operating-period conditions to release of guarantees, contingent equity or similar support. Apply
only the executed criteria.

### T2-S10-436 — test: ppp_public_support_funding_vs_revenue_classification
