### T2-S10-650 — test: energy_efficiency_adjusted_baseline
Recalculate a sample adjusted baseline under the agreed M&V methodology and confirm reporting-period operating conditions are applied consistently without post-hoc bias.

### T2-S10-651 — test: energy_efficiency_mandv_protocol
Map the contract's M&V protocol from actual use and approved adjustments through verified quantity and monetary savings, guarantee comparison, true-up and shortfall settlement.

### T2-S10-652 — test: energy_efficiency_savings_to_cash
Bridge engineering energy savings to avoided tariff cost, contractual guaranteed savings, verified savings, cash available for payment and retained host benefit. Flag mismatched units/perimeters.

### T2-S10-653 — test: energy_efficiency_energy_vs_dollar_guarantee
Determine whether the guarantee is in energy quantity or monetary savings and identify who bears tariff/commodity-price risk, including any floor/cap or valuation convention.

### T2-S10-654 — test: energy_efficiency_shared_savings
For shared-savings arrangements, verify the sharing base, measurement frequency, allocation percentage, settlement timing, floor/cap and treatment of disputed/adjusted savings.

### T2-S10-655 — test: energy_efficiency_ecm_interactions
Test whether bundled ECM savings account for interactions between measures rather than summing standalone estimates that share the same load reduction.

### T2-S10-656 — test: energy_efficiency_commissioning_savings_start
Confirm savings, service payments and guarantees begin only when the applicable installation, commissioning, acceptance and operational triggers are satisfied.

### T2-S10-657 — test: energy_efficiency_om_persistence
Reconcile required O&M, training, monitoring and maintenance capability to the persistence of modeled savings and any ESCO guarantee obligations.

### T2-S10-658 — test: energy_efficiency_pda_development_cost
Review the Project Development Agreement or equivalent for study scope, customer data obligations, development fee/reimbursement, cancellation rights and conversion into the final implementation contract.

### T2-S10-659 — test: energy_efficiency_performance_compensation
Classify compensation as guaranteed dollar savings, guaranteed energy savings, fixed/shared savings, service charge or hybrid and confirm the model follows that economic structure.

### T2-S10-660 — test: energy_efficiency_shortfall_cure
Test the contractual remedy for a savings shortfall, including cash cure, service credit, extra work, term extension or buyout, and confirm timing/caps are modeled.

### T2-S10-661 — test: energy_efficiency_payment_trigger
Reconcile the first payment and ongoing payment dates to possession, acceptance, verified performance or other contractual conditions; test a delayed-acceptance case.

### T2-S10-662 — test: energy_efficiency_cost_of_delay
Quantify the supported cost of deferral using avoidable operating cost, financing cost avoided, asset-condition consequences and incentive expiry where applicable rather than assuming immediate implementation is always superior.

### T2-S10-663 — test: energy_efficiency_financing_structure_fit
Compare financing structures on term, payment shape, collateral, guarantee, residual/ownership, tax/accounting treatment and risk allocation rather than nominal interest rate alone.

### T2-S10-664 — test: energy_efficiency_service_vs_asset_ownership
Identify who owns the ECM/distributed-energy assets during and after the contract and who bears maintenance, performance, tax, residual and removal obligations.

### T2-S10-665 — test: energy_efficiency_mesa_cash_perimeter
For a managed energy service agreement, map host payment, provider-paid utility bill, savings capture and asset ownership to prevent duplicate utility expense or savings.

### T2-S10-666 — test: energy_efficiency_pace_program_eligibility
Where property-linked assessment finance is used, verify current jurisdiction/program eligibility, eligible costs, assessment term, lien/security, transfer and repayment rules against source documents.

### T2-S10-667 — test: energy_efficiency_pace_transfer_default
Test sale, refinancing, non-payment and foreclosure scenarios under the actual property-linked programme rather than assuming the assessment automatically transfers or accelerates.

### T2-S10-668 — test: energy_efficiency_pace_warehouse_refinance
Where assessments are aggregated/warehoused, test origination pace, interim funding cost, take-out/refinancing timing, rate risk and minimum pool/issuance requirements.

### T2-S10-669 — test: energy_efficiency_mortgage_lien_consent
Reconcile any property assessment, fixture security or additional financing to existing mortgage covenants, required lender consent and intercreditor/security priority.

### T2-S10-670 — test: energy_efficiency_split_incentive
For leased property, map utility payer, owner/tenant capex burden, pass-through rights, lease expiry and benefit allocation. Confirm the financing mechanism actually resolves rather than assumes away split incentives.

### T2-S10-671 — test: energy_efficiency_lender_obligor_credit
Where host credit is the primary repayment source, test liquidity, cash-flow/debt-service capacity and payment obligations relevant to the energy contract, distinct from technical savings performance.

### T2-S10-672 — test: energy_efficiency_construction_completion
Review installation/construction funding for milestone approval, bonds/guarantees/retainage, title, lien waivers, insurance, commissioning and acceptance protections.

### T2-S10-673 — test: energy_efficiency_counterparty_credit
Assess ESCO/EPC/O&M provider financial capacity, relevant track record and replacement/step-in arrangements against the obligations they guarantee.

### T2-S10-674 — test: energy_efficiency_technology_risk
Test whether technology performance, useful life, serviceability, obsolescence and maintenance assumptions are supported by the actual equipment/system evidence.

### T2-S10-675 — test: energy_efficiency_performance_credit_boundary
Separate host/obligor payment credit risk from ECM performance risk and identify where a performance dispute can legally suspend debt-service cash.

### T2-S10-676 — test: energy_efficiency_payment_lockbox
Where a lockbox or controlled account exists, reconcile host receipts through required O&M/reserve/debt payments and residual distributions using the actual account agreement.

### T2-S10-677 — test: energy_efficiency_security_fixture
Determine whether financed equipment is personal property, fixture or inseparable building plant and test security perfection, access/removal rights and credible residual value.

### T2-S10-678 — test: energy_efficiency_termination_makewhole
For early termination, reconcile the contractual termination amount/schedule to outstanding financing, break/make-whole, service close-out and asset transfer/removal.

### T2-S10-679 — test: energy_efficiency_assignment_transfer
Review financier/ESCO/host assignment and transfer rights, consent requirements and continuity of service/payment obligations under sale or financing transfer.

### T2-S10-680 — test: energy_efficiency_savings_insurance
Where savings/performance insurance exists, test covered shortfall, deductible, cap, exclusions, claim evidence, verifier requirements, term and interaction with ESCO guarantees.

### T2-S10-681 — test: energy_efficiency_solar_ppa_parties
Map host/site owner, PPA sponsor/SPE, EPC contractor, utility/interconnection party, lenders/equity and incentive recipient, including the site lease/licence and key contractual interfaces.

### T2-S10-682 — test: energy_efficiency_solar_host_economics
Bridge metered solar generation to avoided utility cost, PPA payment and retained host savings, including demand/minimum/non-bypassable charges that remain material.

### T2-S10-683 — test: energy_efficiency_solar_sponsor_economics
Bridge PPA revenue and committed incentives through EPC/development cost, O&M, insurance, lifecycle replacement, financing and tax to sponsor cash return without assigning sponsor benefits to the host.

### T2-S10-684 — test: energy_efficiency_solar_tariff_hedge
Compare PPA rate/escalator with the host's avoided utility tariff over time and identify basis risk where tariff components or escalation drivers differ.

### T2-S10-685 — test: energy_efficiency_solar_site_constraints
Review roof/land/canopy life, structural/site condition, shading/orientation, permits, access, cable/interconnection distance and alternative-use constraints that can affect capex, schedule or output.

### T2-S10-686 — test: energy_efficiency_solar_generation_lifecycle
Reconcile installed capacity through resource, system losses, degradation, availability, inverter/electrical losses, curtailment/export limits and lifecycle replacement to metered energy.

### T2-S10-687 — test: energy_efficiency_incentive_award
For each grant/rebate/tax/environmental support item, verify eligibility, award status, recipient, amount/basis, milestone, timing, expiry and clawback before treating it as project cash.

### T2-S10-688 — test: energy_efficiency_ancillary_benefits
Test each modeled maintenance, resilience, water, compliance, productivity or other ancillary benefit for incremental evidence and prevent generic percentage uplifts or double counting.

### T2-S10-689 — test: energy_efficiency_avoided_capex
Where a retrofit defers/replaces host capex, reconcile the avoided asset, remaining life, timing and amount to the host capital plan and remove any duplicate future replacement.

### T2-S10-690 — test: energy_efficiency_property_value
If energy improvements increase property value, reconcile the value mechanism to incremental NOI/cap rate or supported market evidence and prevent double counting with exit valuation.

### T2-S10-691 — test: energy_efficiency_project_barriers
Identify whether a positive engineering case is blocked by capital budget, credit/covenant, collateral, lease, mortgage consent, M&V, maintenance, utility/interconnection or transaction-cost constraints.

### T2-S10-692 — test: energy_efficiency_current_legal_accounting
For lease/PPA/MESA/PACE/off-balance-sheet or tax claims, require current jurisdictional accounting/legal/tax support rather than relying on historical labels or 2012-era rules.

### T2-S10-693 — test: power_units_capacity_factor
Verify kW/MW and kWh/MWh/GJ conversions, maximum theoretical energy and capacity-factor definitions. Confirm unit scaling and period hours are dimensionally correct.

### T2-S10-694 — test: power_generation_loss_stack
Trace resource/dispatch through gross generation, outage, plant/electrical losses, auxiliary use, curtailment and metered export. Reconcile billed energy to the correct point in the chain.

### T2-S10-695 — test: power_forced_planned_resource_unavailability
Separate forced outage, planned maintenance and resource/dispatch unavailability, test overlap treatment and confirm discrete major outages affect both generation and cost.

### T2-S10-696 — test: power_fuel_calorific_efficiency
For fuelled plants, reconcile delivered fuel price, calorific/heating value and efficiency/heat rate to fuel quantity and variable cost per net MWh.

### T2-S10-697 — test: power_fixed_variable_om
Classify O&M by period/capacity, MWh, operating hour/start, consumable or lifecycle driver and reconcile each cost to plant operations.

### T2-S10-698 — test: power_construction_real_nominal_phasing
Bridge dated real/base-price development/EPC/interconnection costs through relevant escalation to nominal/MOD spend, funding, IDC/fees and COD.

### T2-S10-699 — test: power_tariff_energy_capacity_components
Separate energy, capacity/standing, availability, fuel pass-through, incentive and other tariff components and confirm the correct physical/payment driver for each.

### T2-S10-700 — test: power_tariff_reverse_bankability
Reverse-solve the tariff/payment required for a defined lender/equity target and compare it with executed/allowed/market/affordable tariff. Treat any gap as a bankability issue.

### T2-S10-701 — test: power_incentive_streams_current
Verify renewable/environmental certificate, grant or other support eligibility, recipient, term, indexation and expiry against current project evidence.

### T2-S10-702 — test: power_project_equity_blended_return
Recalculate project, ordinary-equity, shareholder-debt and blended sponsor returns on consistent real/nominal and tax bases with clear instrument cash-flow perimeters.

### T2-S10-703 — test: power_sensitivity_physical_driver_set
Confirm downside cases flex the material retained physical/economic risks such as resource, outage, heat rate, fuel, O&M, lifecycle, tariff, grid and financing rather than generic isolated percentages.

### T2-S10-704 — test: power_ccgt_cycle_efficiency
For CCGT/gas-fired projects, trace gas turbine and steam/heat-recovery cycle output, fuel/heat-rate, auxiliary/cooling, outage and overhaul to net export and PPA economics.

### T2-S10-705 — test: power_coal_fuel_ash_environment
For coal projects, trace fuel quality/calorific value and heat rate through generation and include material handling, ash/residue, environmental consumables/compliance and outage costs.

### T2-S10-706 — test: power_efw_feedstock_gatefee
For waste-to-energy, reconcile accepted waste tonnes, calorific value/throughput, gate fee or feedstock cost, residue disposal/recovery and resulting power/heat output.

### T2-S10-707 — test: power_solar_thermal_resource_chain
For solar thermal/CSP, trace irradiation through collector/thermal system, storage if any, steam/power conversion, parasitic loss and O&M to net export.

### T2-S10-708 — test: power_hydro_hydrology_runofriver
For hydro/run-of-river, reconcile hydrology/inflow, head/flow, environmental flow, turbine efficiency, seasonality and operating rules to generation.

### T2-S10-709 — test: power_pumped_storage_energy_balance
For pumped storage, reconcile pumping energy, storage/round-trip loss, reservoir constraints and discharge revenue/capacity services; reject free-energy treatment.

### T2-S10-710 — test: power_tidal_wave_seasonal_marine
For tidal/wave assets, test seasonal resource, device availability, marine access, subsea/export system and major maintenance against generation and cost.

### T2-S10-711 — test: power_wind_onshore_resource_outage
For onshore wind, reconcile resource study/power curve through wake/electrical losses, availability, curtailment and lifecycle to net metered energy.

### T2-S10-712 — test: power_wind_offshore_marine_om_grid
For offshore wind, add marine foundations, vessel/weather access, array/export cable/substation, grid, corrosion and major-component replacement to the wind generation bridge.

### T2-S10-713 — test: power_technology_capex_per_kw_reasonableness
Calculate capex per kW/MW by consistent scope and price date and use it only as a normalized reasonableness metric, not a textbook pass/fail benchmark.

### T2-S10-714 — test: power_annual_unit_cost_sensecheck
Calculate revenue, fuel, variable O&M, fixed O&M and lifecycle cost per MWh/kWh over time and investigate unexplained discontinuities or divergence from input drivers.

### T2-S10-715 — test: power_working_capital_tariff_receivable
Where material, reconcile PPA/tariff billing, receivable days, operating payables and surplus/restricted cash to the actual settlement cycle and lender cash waterfall.

### T2-S10-716 — test: power_debt_service_periodicity
Confirm model periodicity is fine enough for construction/resource/payment timing and aligned to actual debt-service dates; do not impose semi-annual periods where the documents differ.

### T2-S10-717 — test: power_lender_ratio_document_definition
Trace DSCR/ADSCR, LLCR and PLCR numerator, denominator, reserve treatment, periods and target thresholds to executed finance documents for the power project.

### T2-S10-718 — test: power_tariff_sensitivity_credit_bridge
Trace a power downside from physical driver through metered output/tariff, CFADS and lender ratios, showing contractual pass-through/guarantees before concluding on credit impact.

### T2-S10-719 — test: power_data_book_contract_trace
Map each material resource, plant, PPA, EPC/O&M, fuel, interconnection and financing assumption to its technical/data-book/contract source and model location.

### T2-S10-720 — test: power_operating_outage_lifecycle
Verify major planned outage and component replacement schedules are reflected simultaneously in availability/generation, O&M/lifecycle capex, reserves and covenant cash flows.

### T2-S10-721 — test: power_merchant_vs_supported_revenue
Separate contracted/support revenue from merchant energy/capacity/ancillary revenue and ensure merchant assumptions begin only where contractual support ends or allows market exposure.

### T2-S10-722 — test: realestate_strategy_perimeter
Classify develop-and-sell, staged sale, build-to-rent/hold, develop-to-core/refinance, mixed sell/hold or refurbishment and confirm revenue, valuation and terminal proceeds match that strategy.

### T2-S10-723 — test: realestate_site_control_land_cash
Reconcile option/conditional contract/deposit/acquisition/ground-lease or vendor-finance site control to legal ownership, land cash timing and forfeiture/extension rights.

### T2-S10-724 — test: realestate_planning_entitlement_density
Trace planning/entitlement status and conditions to developable area/unit count, timing, authority contributions and land/development value.

### T2-S10-725 — test: realestate_gdv_unit_reconciliation
Reconcile GDV/NDV by unit/area/product to price/rent assumptions, incentives, selling costs and settlement/valuation timing.

### T2-S10-726 — test: realestate_residual_land_value
Reperform residual land value from development value less development costs and required return on the exact convention used; cross-check against market/alternative-use evidence where available.

### T2-S10-727 — test: realestate_valuation_vs_appraisal
Confirm the model distinguishes market valuation/basis-of-value from developer feasibility and does not present an internal hurdle as observed market value.

### T2-S10-728 — test: realestate_static_residual_vs_dcf
For material multi-period developments, compare static residual conclusions to timed DCF and identify duration/finance-carry differences.

### T2-S10-729 — test: realestate_profit_metric_definition
Verify profit on cost, profit on value, project IRR, equity IRR and MOIC use explicit, consistent numerators/denominators and are not compared interchangeably.

### T2-S10-730 — test: realestate_return_duration_effect
Test the implied annualised return of stated cash-margin hurdles across actual project duration/staging and flag counter-intuitive duration effects.

### T2-S10-731 — test: realestate_development_cost_stack
Reconcile land, hard/soft costs, authority contributions, infrastructure, marketing/leasing, holding costs, finance fees/interest, contingency and close-out costs to current scope.

### T2-S10-732 — test: realestate_cost_timing_finance_carry
Trace monthly/quarterly cost phasing and receipts to actual debt draws and capitalised interest rather than average-cost shortcuts.

### T2-S10-733 — test: realestate_adc_facility_binding_constraints
Size construction debt against actual commitment, LTC, LTV, equity, interest-reserve, presale/prelease and cost-to-complete constraints and identify the binding limit.

### T2-S10-734 — test: realestate_equity_funding_sequence
Confirm borrower equity is funded first/pro-rata/pari-passu or otherwise exactly as required by finance documents.

### T2-S10-735 — test: realestate_cost_to_complete
Compare current remaining committed funds with latest cost-to-complete including finance carry, contingency, taxes, lease-up and completion costs.

### T2-S10-736 — test: realestate_interest_reserve
Reconcile opening reserve, funded interest, cash interest from project, reserve draw, replenishment and remaining adequacy through completion/stabilization.

### T2-S10-737 — test: realestate_interest_reserve_masking
Flag continued/repacked reserve draws after the property should be cash-generative unless current feasibility and borrower support have been re-underwritten.

### T2-S10-738 — test: realestate_developer_capacity
Assess sponsor/developer track record, liquidity, competing commitments, guarantees and ability to fund overruns relative to the modeled support case.

### T2-S10-739 — test: realestate_construction_draw_controls
Trace budget, certified progress, equity contribution, lender draw, contractor payment, changes/retention and updated cost-to-complete.

### T2-S10-740 — test: realestate_presale_quality
Segment reservations, signed contracts, conditional/unconditional sales, deposits and settlements; apply cancellation/default/finance-condition risk appropriately.

### T2-S10-741 — test: realestate_leaseup_cash_conversion
Bridge completed/available area to lease signed, possession, rent commencement, incentives, billed rent, collections and economic occupancy.

### T2-S10-742 — test: realestate_absorption_price_correlation
Stress slower absorption with realistic price/rent incentives, longer carry and exit/refinance delay rather than independent one-variable cases.

### T2-S10-743 — test: realestate_stabilization_takeout
Test construction/bridge debt refinance against actual stabilization/take-out conditions including occupancy, NOI, DSCR, debt yield, LTV and tenant quality.

### T2-S10-744 — test: realestate_noi_definition
Reconcile rental income, vacancy, concessions, bad debt, operating costs and capital exclusions to lender-defined NOI.

### T2-S10-745 — test: realestate_dscr_debt_yield_ltv
Reperform stabilized property DSCR, debt yield and LTV on lender-defined NOI/value and test covenant/takeout headroom.

### T2-S10-746 — test: realestate_value_state_bridge
Distinguish as-is, as-complete, as-stabilized and liquidation/workout values and trace assumptions required to move between them.

### T2-S10-747 — test: realestate_mixed_use_allocation
Reconcile component revenues/absorption and shared land, parking, public realm, utilities and common infrastructure allocation without internal double counting.

### T2-S10-748 — test: realestate_exit_value
Reperform sale/terminal value from stabilized NOI or supported comparable method, forward cap/discount rate, transaction costs, taxes and timing.

### T2-S10-749 — test: realestate_refinance_capacity
Re-size future refinance using future rates, NOI, DSCR/debt-yield/LTV and value rather than assuming repayment equals outstanding construction debt.

### T2-S10-750 — test: realestate_mezz_pref_holdco
Trace mezzanine/preferred/holdco debt cash-pay/PIK, maturity, control and repayment to distributions available after senior project constraints.

### T2-S10-751 — test: realestate_correlated_downside
Run at least one combined cost overrun + delay + weaker value/absorption + weaker take-out financing case where material.

### T2-S10-752 — test: bess_power_energy_duration
Reconcile MW, usable MWh, SOC limits, duration, charge/discharge limits and interconnection constraints.

### T2-S10-753 — test: bess_soc_rollforward
Reperform SOC continuity including charge/discharge efficiency, auxiliary/self-discharge where material and minimum/maximum SOC.

### T2-S10-754 — test: bess_round_trip_efficiency
Verify efficiency convention is applied once and energy losses reconcile between charging and discharge.

### T2-S10-755 — test: bess_revenue_stack_compatibility
Confirm stacked services respect MW reservation, exclusivity, SOC headroom, market eligibility and simultaneous-use rules.

### T2-S10-756 — test: bess_availability_activation_revenue
Separate ancillary-service availability/capacity payment from activation/response-energy payment using actual market terms.

### T2-S10-757 — test: bess_degradation_dispatch_link
Trace dispatch/throughput/cycles/SOC strategy to degradation or justify the chosen simplified degradation model.

### T2-S10-758 — test: bess_augmentation_replacement
Reconcile degradation to augmentation/replacement capex, outage and restored contracted usable MWh/MW.

### T2-S10-759 — test: bess_warranty_constraints
Compare modeled cycles, throughput, SOC, C-rate/temperature assumptions and capacity retention with OEM warranty limits.

### T2-S10-760 — test: bess_contracted_merchant_revenue
Classify storage revenue by contract tenor/counterparty and ensure lender debt sizing does not treat merchant stacked revenue as contracted.

### T2-S10-761 — test: bess_market_saturation_downside
Stress merchant/ancillary-service price compression, saturation/rule changes and impact on debt/equity returns.

### T2-S10-762 — test: bess_hybrid_energy_balance
For co-located renewables, reconcile renewable output, clipping, charging source, BESS losses and shared export/interconnection limit without double-counted MWh.

### T2-S10-763 — test: bess_grid_charge_eligibility
Test whether grid charging changes tariff, tax/incentive, certificate or contract eligibility and economics.

### T2-S10-764 — test: bess_end_of_life
Model end-of-life residual/recycling/decommissioning or replacement obligations where material.

### T2-S10-765 — test: airport_aeronautical_cost_base
Reconcile aeronautical operating cost, depreciation/asset consumption, allowed financing/capital return and shared cost allocation to the chargeable cost base.

### T2-S10-766 — test: airport_charge_unit_definition
Reperform landing/passenger/parking/security/cargo charges on the exact movement, MTOW, passenger or other contractual/regulatory unit.

### T2-S10-767 — test: airport_transfer_exempt_traffic
Check transfer/transit/exempt passenger and movement classes are excluded/included correctly for each charge.

### T2-S10-768 — test: airport_tax_vs_charge
Separate government taxes/levies from airport charges and retain only cash legally available to the airport.

### T2-S10-769 — test: airport_charge_approval_path
Test whether modeled charge increases require consultation/regulatory/user approval and run timing/downside if approval is not automatic.

### T2-S10-770 — test: airport_nonaero_concession_structure
Reconcile fixed rent/minimum guarantee/turnover share or direct-operated commercial revenue and associated costs.

### T2-S10-771 — test: airport_network_cross_subsidy
Where an airport network exists, trace central costs, transfers and cross-subsidies between profitable and supported airports.

### T2-S10-772 — test: airport_prefunding_capex
If charges pre-finance future infrastructure, trace ring-fencing/escrow, approved project timing and reconciliation/refund requirements.

### T2-S10-773 — test: airport_expansion_financing
Bridge traffic and charge-setting rights through aeronautical/non-aeronautical cash flow, capex, reserves and debt service.

### T2-S10-774 — test: lender_es_categorisation_scope
Where Equator/IFC-style requirements apply, identify E&S category and resulting diligence/monitoring scope without converting category into an arbitrary valuation haircut.

### T2-S10-775 — test: lender_es_action_plan_cost
Trace material E&S action-plan items to responsible party, capex/opex, schedule, financing condition and remedy.

### T2-S10-776 — test: lender_es_resettlement_access
Reconcile resettlement/compensation/community-access obligations to land possession, budget and construction schedule.

### T2-S10-777 — test: lender_es_independent_monitoring
Identify independent E&S review/monitoring costs and any certification required for drawdown/completion/continuing compliance.

### T2-S10-778 — test: lender_es_covenant_breach
Run the contractual cure/drawstop/lock-up/remediation/default path for a material E&S covenant breach.

### T2-S10-779 — test: climate_resilience_incremental_cost
Bridge base technical design to incremental resilience capex/opex/maintenance and financing cost.

### T2-S10-780 — test: climate_resilience_benefit_recipient
Identify whether resilience benefits are private cash, public fiscal savings, economic avoided loss or service-continuity benefits and prevent non-cash benefits entering CFADS.

### T2-S10-781 — test: climate_resilience_funding_source
Verify committed equity/debt/public/concessional/grant/guarantee/insurance funding for incremental resilience costs.

### T2-S10-782 — test: climate_resilience_maintenance
Test routine/lifecycle maintenance is sufficient to preserve resilience performance through asset life.

### T2-S10-783 — test: climate_data_maturity
Compare early hazard screen to current engineering/financial-close climate assessment and reforecast material changes in exposure/adaptation.

### T2-S10-784 — test: climate_contract_allocation
Separate controllable resilience duties, ordinary weather, foreseeable adaptation and exceptional climate relief/force-majeure risks according to contract.

### T2-S10-785 — test: climate_disaster_liquidity
Model post-disaster repair cash, insurance deductible/waiting period, reserves/contingent credit and debt service timing.

### T2-S10-786 — test: specialised_lending_rating_evidence
Where a bank uses specialised-lending slotting/internal grades, reconcile financial strength, legal/regulatory, transaction, sponsor/developer and security evidence to the assigned rating category.

### T2-S10-787 — test: specialised_lending_model_not_grade_plug
Confirm the model case is not altered merely to achieve a preferred lender rating/slotting category; rating should follow evidence and stress results.
